 // Parse the agent's response.\n206\t        let response = parse_mitosis_response(&exec_result.stdout);\n207\t\n208\t        match response {\n209\t            Some(resp) if resp.splittable && !resp.children.is_empty() => {\n210\t                self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n211\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n212\t                    splittable: true,\n213\t                    proposed_children: resp.children.len() as u32,\n214\t                })?;\n215\t\n216\t                self.create_children(store, bead, &resp.children).await\n217\t            }\n218\t            Some(resp) if resp.splittable => {\n219\t                // Splittable but no children proposed — treat as not splittable.\n220\t                tracing::info!(\n221\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n222\t                    \"agent said splittable but proposed no children\"\n223\t                );\n224\t                self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n225\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n226\t                    splittable: false,\n227\t                    proposed_children: 0,\n228\t                })?;\n229\t                Ok(MitosisResult::NotSplittable)\n230\t            }\n231\t            Some(_) => {\n232\t                tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent determined bead is single task\");\n233\t                self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n234\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n235\t                    splittable: false,\n236\t                    proposed_children: 0,\n237\t                })?;\n238\t                Ok(MitosisResult::NotSplittable)\n239\t            }\n240\t            None => {\n241\t                tracing::warn!(\n242\t                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n243\t                    exit_code = exec_result.exit_code,\n244\t                    \"could not parse mitosis response from agent\"\n245\t                );\n246\t                self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n247\t                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n248\t                    splittable: false,\n249\t                    proposed_children: 0,\n250\t                })?;\n251\t                Ok(MitosisResult::NotSplittable)\n252\t            }\n253\t        }\n254\t    }\n255\t\n256\t    /// Create child beads with dedup against existing children.\n257\t    async fn create_children(\n258\t        &self,\n259\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n260\t        parent: &Bead,\n261\t        proposed: &[ProposedChild],\n262\t    ) -> Result<MitosisResult> {\n263\t        // Enter the bead.mitosis span for the mitosis operation.\n264\t        let mitosis_span = tracing::info_span!(\n265\t            \"bead.mitosis\",\n266\t            needle.bead.id = %parent.id,\n267\t            needle.mitosis.proposed_children = proposed.len() as u32,\n268\t            needle.mitosis.children_created = tracing::field::Empty, // Will be set based on result\n269\t            needle.mitosis.children_skipped = tracing::field::Empty, // Will be set based on result\n270\t        );\n271\t        let _mitosis_enter = mitosis_span.enter();\n272\t\n273\t        // Read parent's existing children (dependencies where child blocks parent).\n274\t        let existing = self.get_existing_children(store, &parent.id).await?;\n275\t        let existing_titles: Vec<String> = existing.iter().map(|t| t.to_lowercase()).collect();\n276\t\n277\t        let mut created_ids = Vec::new();\n278\t        let mut skipped = 0u32;\n279\t\n280\t        for child in proposed {\n281\t            // Dedup: does an existing child cover this task?\n282\t            if existing_titles\n283\t                .iter()\n284\t                .any(|t| titles_match(t, &child.title.to_lowercase()))\n285\t            {\n286\t                tracing::debug!(\n287\t                    parent_id = %parent.id,\n288\t                    child_title = %child.title,\n289\t                    \"skipping duplicate child\"\n290\t                );\n291\t                skipped += 1;\n292\t                continue;\n293\t            }\n294\t\n295\t            // Create child bead with parent-tracking labels for reliable dedup.\n296\t            // Labels are stored on the bead itself and survive FrankenSQLite\n297\t            // index corruption, unlike dependency relationships.\n298\t            let parent_label = format!(\"parent-{}\", parent.id);\n299\t            let labels: Vec<&str> = vec![\"mitosis-child\", \"mitosis-depth:1\", &parent_label];\n300\t            let child_id = store\n301\t                .create_bead(&child.title, &child.body, &labels)\n302\t                .await\n303\t                .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to create child bead: {}\", child.title))?;\n304\t\n305\t            // Link child as blocking parent.\n306\t            store\n307\t                .add_dependency(&child_id, &parent.id)\n308\t                .await\n309\t                .with_context(|| {\n310\t                    format!(\n311\t                        \"failed to add dependency: {} blocks {}\",\n312\t                        child_id, parent.id\n313\t                    )\n314\t                })?;\n315\t\n316\t            tracing::info!(\n317\t                parent_id = %parent.id,\n318\t                child_id = %child_id,\n319\t                child_title = %child.title,\n320\t                \"created mitosis child\"\n321\t            );\n322\t\n323\t            created_ids.push(child_id);\n324\t        }\n325\t\n326\t        if created_ids.is_empty() {\n327\t            // All proposed children already existed.\n328\t            tracing::info!(\n329\t                parent_id = %parent.id,\n330\t                existing = existing.len(),\n331\t                \"all proposed children already exist (dedup)\"\n332\t            );\n333\t            self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisSkipped {\n334\t                parent_id: parent.id.clone(),\n335\t                existing_children: existing.len() as u32,\n336\t            })?;\n337\t            return Ok(MitosisResult::Skipped {\n338\t                reason: \"all children already exist\".to_string(),\n339\t            });\n340\t        }\n341\t\n342\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisSplit {\n343\t            parent_id: parent.id.clone(),\n344\t            children_created: created_ids.len() as u32,\n345\t            children_skipped: skipped,\n346\t            child_ids: created_ids.clone(),\n347\t        })?;\n348\t\n349\t        // Record the final counts on the bead.mitosis span\n350\t        tracing::Span::current()\n351\t            .record(\"needle.mitosis.children_created\", created_ids.len() as u32);\n352\t        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.mitosis.children_skipped\", skipped);\n353\t\n354\t        tracing::info!(\n355\t            parent_id = %parent.id,\n356\t            children_created = created_ids.len(),\n357\t            children_skipped = skipped,\n358\t            \"mitosis split completed\"\n359\t        );\n360\t\n361\t        Ok(MitosisResult::Split {\n362\t            children: created_ids,\n363\t        })\n364\t    }\n365\t\n366\t    /// Read the failure count label from a bead.\n367\t    ///\n368\t    /// All br calls are wrapped in timeouts to prevent indefinite hang.\n369\t    async fn get_failure_count(&self, store: &dyn BeadStore, bead_id: &BeadId) -> Result<u32> {\n370\t        let labels =\n371\t            match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), store.labels(bead_id))\n372\t                .await\n373\t            {\n374\t                Ok(Ok(l)) => l,\n375\t                Ok(Err(e)) => {\n376\t                    tracing::warn!(\n377\t                        bead_id = %bead_id,\n378\t                        error = %e,\n379\t                        \"failed to read labels for failure count\"\n380\t                    );\n381\t                    return Ok(0);\n382\t                }\n383\t                Err(_) => {\n384\t                    tracing::warn!(\n385\t                        bead_id = %bead_id,\n386\t                        \"labels() timed out after 30s, assuming failure count 0\"\n387\t                    );\n388\t                    return Ok(0);\n389\t                }\n390\t            };\n391\t\n392\t        let count = labels\n393\t            .iter()\n394\t            .filter_map(|l| l.strip_prefix(\"failure-count:\"))\n395\t            .filter_map(|n| n.parse::<u32>().ok())\n396\t            .max()\n397\t            .unwrap_or(0);\n398\t\n399\t        Ok(count)\n400\t    }\n401\t\n402\t    /// Get titles of existing children for a parent bead.\n403\t    ///\n404\t    /// Uses label-based discovery (`parent-<parent_id>` label) instead of\n405\t    /// reading the parent's dependency list. This is robust against\n406\t    /// FrankenSQLite index corruption where `br dep add` creates the\n407\t    /// dependency link and labels but the relationship doesn't appear in\n408\t    /// `br show --json` output.\n409\t    ///\n410\t    /// The `list_all()` call is wrapped in a timeout to prevent indefinite\n411\t    /// hang in HANDLING state.\n412\t    async fn get_existing_children(\n413\t        &self,\n414\t        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n415\t        parent_id: &BeadId,\n416\t    ) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n417\t        let parent_label = format!(\"parent-{}\", parent_id);\n418\t        let all_beads = match tokio::time::timeout(\n419\t            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n420\t            store.list_all(),\n421\t        )\n422\t        .await\n423\t        {\n424\t            Ok(Ok(beads)) => beads,\n425\t            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n426\t                tracing::warn!(\n427\t                    parent_id = %parent_id,\n428\t                    error = %e,\n429\t                    \"list_all failed during get_existing_children, assuming no children\"\n430\t                );\n431\t                return Ok(Vec::new());\n432\t            }\n433\t            Err(_) => {\n434\t                tracing::warn!(\n435\t                    parent_id = %parent_id,\n436\t                    \"list_all timed out after 30s during get_existing_children, assuming no children\"\n437\t                );\n438\t                return Ok(Vec::new());\n439\t            }\n440\t        };\n441\t\n442\t        let titles: Vec<String> = all_beads\n443\t            .iter()\n444\t            .filter(|b| b.labels.iter().any(|l| l == &parent_label))\n445\t            .map(|b| b.title.clone())\n446\t            .collect();\n447\t\n448\t        Ok(titles)\n449\t    }\n450\t}\n451\t\n452\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n453\t// Parsing\n454\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n455\t\n456\t/// Parse the agent's mitosis analysis response from stdout.\n457\t///\n458\t/// Searches for a JSON object in the output. Handles markdown code fencing\n459\t/// and surrounding text.\n460\tfn parse_mitosis_response(stdout: &str) -> Option<MitosisResponse> {\n461\t    // Try direct JSON parse first.\n462\t    if let Ok(resp) = serde_json::from_str::<MitosisResponse>(stdout.trim()) {\n463\t        return Some(resp);\n464\t    }\n465\t\n466\t    // Try extracting JSON from markdown code fences.\n467\t    let json_str = extract_json_block(stdout)?;\n468\t    serde_json::from_str::<MitosisResponse>(json_str).ok()\n469\t}\n470\t\n471\t/// Extract a JSON object from text that may contain markdown code fences.\n472\tfn extract_json_block(text: &str) -> Option<&str> {\n473\t    // Look for ```json ... ``` or ``` ... ``` blocks.\n474\t    if let Some(start) = text.find(\"```json\") {\n475\t        let content_start = start + \"```json\".len();\n476\t        if let Some(end) = text[content_start..].find(\"```\") {\n477\t            return Some(text[content_start..content_start + end].trim());\n478\t        }\n479\t    }\n480\t\n481\t    if let Some(start) = text.find(\"```\") {\n482\t        let content_start = start + \"```\".len();\n483\t        // Skip to next line if the opening ``` has text after it.\n484\t        let content_start = text[content_start..]\n485\t            .find('\\n')\n486\t            .map(|n| content_start + n + 1)\n487\t            .unwrap_or(content_start);\n488\t        if let Some(end) = text[content_start..].find(\"```\") {\n489\t            return Some(text[content_start..content_start + end].trim());\n490\t        }\n491\t    }\n492\t\n493\t    // Try to find a bare JSON object.\n494\t    let trimmed = text.trim();\n495\t    if trimmed.starts_with('{') {\n496\t        // Find matching closing brace (simple heuristic).\n497\t        let mut depth = 0i32;\n498\t        for (i, ch) in trimmed.char_indices() {\n499\t            match ch {\n500\t                '{' => depth += 1,\n501\t                '}' => {\n502\t                    depth -= 1;\n503\t                    if depth == 0 {\n504\t                        return Some(&trimmed[..=i]);\n505\t                    }\n506\t                }\n507\t                _ => {}\n508\t            }\n509\t        }\n510\t    }\n511\t\n512\t    None\n513\t}\n514\t\n515\t/// Sanitize a path string for use as a filename component.\n516\tfn sanitize_path_component(s: &str) -> String {\n517\t    s.chars()\n518\t        .map(|c| {\n519\t            if c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '-' || c == '_' {\n520\t                c\n521\t            } else {\n522\t                '_'\n523\t            }\n524\t        })\n525\t        .collect()\n526\t}\n527\t\n528\t/// Check if two titles match (fuzzy comparison for dedup).\n529\t///\n530\t/// Considers titles matching if they are identical after normalization,\n531\t/// or if one contains the other as a substring.\n532\tfn titles_match(existing: &str, proposed: &str) -> bool {\n533\t    if existing == proposed {\n534\t        return true;\n535\t    }\n536\t\n537\t    // Normalize: remove common prefixes and compare.\n538\t    let normalize = |s: &str| -> String {\n539\t        s.trim()\n540\t            .trim_start_matches(|c: char| !c.is_alphabetic())\n541\t            .to_lowercase()\n542\t    };\n543\t\n544\t    let e = normalize(existing);\n545\t    let p = normalize(proposed);\n546\t\n547\t    e == p || e.contains(&p) || p.contains(&e)\n548\t}\n549\t\n550\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n551\t// Tests\n552\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n553\t\n554\t#[cfg(test)]\n555\tmod tests {\n556\t    use super::*;\n557\t    use crate::bead_store::{Filters, }\n1245\t}\n1246\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"86297c1c-e9b2-4ff1-bd6d-2d1b721b83a7","uuid":"17df20ca-c649-465a-8922-6c0fa4f3220d","timestamp":"2026-07-03T14:35:29.966Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/coding/NEEDLE/src/mitosis/mod.rs","content":"//! Mitosis: split multi-task beads into focused children on first failure.\n//!\n//! Uses child-aware deduplication to prevent duplicate splits. The parent's\n//! existing children serve as the dedup source — if children already cover\n//! a proposed task, that child is skipped.\n//!\n//! Concurrency safety: a per-workspace flock serializes the entire mitosis\n//! operation (read children → create → link dependencies).\n//!\n//! Depends on: `bead_store`, `config`, `dispatch`, `prompt`, `telemetry`, `types`, `claim`.\n\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\n\nuse crate::bead_store::BeadStore;\nuse crate::claim::acquire_flock;\nuse crate::config::MitosisConfig;\nuse crate::dispatch::Dispatcher;\nuse crate::prompt::PromptBuilder;\nuse crate::telemetry::{EventKind, Telemetry};\nuse crate::types::{Bead, BeadId};\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// MitosisResult\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Outcome of a mitosis evaluation.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum MitosisResult {\n    /// Bead was split into child beads.\n    Split {\n        /// IDs of the newly created children.\n        children: Vec<BeadId>,\n    },\n    /// Agent determined the bead is a single task — no split.\n    NotSplittable,\n    /// Mitosis was skipped (disabled, not first failure, etc.).\n    Skipped { reason: String },\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// ProposedChild\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// A child bead proposed by the agent during mitosis analysis.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Deserialize)]\nstruct ProposedChild {\n    title: String,\n    body: String,\n}\n\n/// Agent's mitosis analysis response.\n#[derive(Debug, serde::Deserialize)]\nstruct MitosisResponse {\n    splittable: bool,\n    #[serde(default)]\n    children: Vec<ProposedChild>,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// MitosisEvaluator\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Evaluates beads for splitting and creates child beads when appropriate.\npub struct MitosisEvaluator {\n    config: MitosisConfig,\n    telemetry: Telemetry,\n    lock_dir: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl MitosisEvaluator {\n    pub fn new(config: MitosisConfig, telemetry: Telemetry, lock_dir: PathBuf) -> Self {\n        MitosisEvaluator {\n            config,\n            telemetry,\n            lock_dir,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Evaluate a bead for mitosis after failure.\n    ///\n    /// Checks preconditions (enabled, first failure), then dispatches the agent\n    /// to analyze whether the bead contains multiple independent tasks.\n    /// If splittable, creates child beads with dedup against existing children.\n    pub async fn evaluate(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        bead: &Bead,\n        workspace: &Path,\n        dispatcher: &Dispatcher,\n        prompt_builder: &PromptBuilder,\n        agent_name: &str,\n    ) -> Result<MitosisResult> {\n        // Check if mitosis is enabled.\n        if !self.config.enabled {\n            tracing::debug!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"mitosis disabled\");\n            return Ok(MitosisResult::Skipped {\n                reason: \"disabled\".to_string(),\n            });\n        }\n\n        // Check failure count conditions.\n        let failure_count = self.get_failure_count(store, &bead.id).await?;\n\n        // force_failure_threshold: trigger only when failure_count reaches the threshold.\n        if self.config.force_failure_threshold > 0 {\n            if failure_count < self.config.force_failure_threshold {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    failure_count,\n                    threshold = self.config.force_failure_threshold,\n                    \"mitosis skipped: below force_failure_threshold\"\n                );\n                return Ok(MitosisResult::Skipped {\n                    reason: format!(\n                        \"failure count {} below threshold {}\",\n                        failure_count, self.config.force_failure_threshold\n                    ),\n                });\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check if we should fire based on first_failure_only or repeat_interval.\n            let should_fire = if self.config.repeat_interval > 0 {\n                // repeat_interval mode: fire at 1, 1+N, 1+2N, ...\n                // But skip beads that are already mitosis children (have mitosis-depth label).\n                let has_mitosis_depth_label =\n                    bead.labels.iter().any(|l| l.starts_with(\"mitosis-depth:\"));\n                let is_repeat_tick =\n                    failure_count > 1 && (failure_count - 1) % self.config.repeat_interval == 0;\n\n                // Fire at first failure OR at repeat interval ticks (if not a mitosis child)\n                failure_count == 1 || (is_repeat_tick && !has_mitosis_depth_label)\n            } else {\n                // first_failure_only mode: only fire at failure_count == 1\n                !self.config.first_failure_only || failure_count == 1\n            };\n\n            if !should_fire {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    failure_count,\n                    first_failure_only = self.config.first_failure_only,\n                    repeat_interval = self.config.repeat_interval,\n                    \"mitosis skipped: not at trigger point\"\n                );\n                return Ok(MitosisResult::Skipped {\n                    reason: format!(\n                        \"not at trigger point (count={}, first_failure_only={}, repeat_interval={})\",\n                        failure_count, self.config.first_failure_only, self.config.repeat_interval\n                    ),\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Resolve the agent adapter.\n        let adapter = match dispatcher.adapter(agent_name) {\n            Some(a) => a,\n            None => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    agent = agent_name,\n                    \"mitosis skipped: agent adapter not found\"\n                );\n                return Ok(MitosisResult::Skipped {\n                    reason: format!(\"adapter '{}' not found\", agent_name),\n                });\n            }\n        };\n\n        // Gather existing children for the prompt (so the agent avoids duplicates).\n        let existing_children = self.get_existing_children(store, &bead.id).await?;\n        let existing_children_text = if existing_children.is_empty() {\n            \"(no existing children)\".to_string()\n        } else {\n            existing_children\n                .iter()\n                .map(|t| format!(\"- {t}\"))\n                .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n                .join(\"\\n\")\n        };\n\n        // Build mitosis prompt.\n        let prompt = prompt_builder\n            .build_mitosis(bead, workspace, \"mitosis\", &existing_children_text)\n            .context(\"failed to build mitosis prompt\")?;\n\n        // Acquire workspace flock for atomicity.\n        let lock_path = self.lock_dir.join(format!(\n            \"needle-mitosis-{}.lock\",\n            sanitize_path_component(&workspace.display().to_string())\n        ));\n        let _lock = acquire_flock(&lock_path)\n            .await\n            .context(\"failed to acquire mitosis flock\")?;\n\n        tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"dispatching agent for mitosis analysis\");\n\n        // Dispatch agent with mitosis prompt.\n        let exec_result = dispatcher\n            .dispatch(&bead.id, &prompt, adapter, workspace)\n            .await\n            .context(\"mitosis agent dispatch failed\")?;\n\n        // Parse the agent's response.\n        let response = parse_mitosis_response(&exec_result.stdout);\n\n        match response {\n            Some(resp) if resp.splittable && !resp.children.is_empty() => {\n                self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    splittable: true,\n                    proposed_children: resp.children.len() as u32,\n                })?;\n\n                self.create_children(store, bead, &resp.children).await\n            }\n            Some(resp) if resp.splittable => {\n                // Splittable but no children proposed — treat as not splittable.\n                tracing::info!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    \"agent said splittable but proposed no children\"\n                );\n                self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    splittable: false,\n                    proposed_children: 0,\n                })?;\n                Ok(MitosisResult::NotSplittable)\n            }\n            Some(_) => {\n                tracing::info!(bead_id = %bead.id, \"agent determined bead is single task\");\n                self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    splittable: false,\n                    proposed_children: 0,\n                })?;\n                Ok(MitosisResult::NotSplittable)\n            }\n            None => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    bead_id = %bead.id,\n                    exit_code = exec_result.exit_code,\n                    \"could not parse mitosis response from agent\"\n                );\n                self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisEvaluated {\n                    bead_id: bead.id.clone(),\n                    splittable: false,\n                    proposed_children: 0,\n                })?;\n                Ok(MitosisResult::NotSplittable)\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Create child beads with dedup against existing children.\n    async fn create_children(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        parent: &Bead,\n        proposed: &[ProposedChild],\n    ) -> Result<MitosisResult> {\n        // Enter the bead.mitosis span for the mitosis operation.\n        let mitosis_span = tracing::info_span!(\n            \"bead.mitosis\",\n            needle.bead.id = %parent.id,\n            needle.mitosis.proposed_children = proposed.len() as u32,\n            needle.mitosis.children_created = tracing::field::Empty, // Will be set based on result\n            needle.mitosis.children_skipped = tracing::field::Empty, // Will be set based on result\n        );\n        let _mitosis_enter = mitosis_span.enter();\n\n        // Read parent's existing children (dependencies where child blocks parent).\n        let existing = self.get_existing_children(store, &parent.id).await?;\n        let existing_titles: Vec<String> = existing.iter().map(|t| t.to_lowercase()).collect();\n\n        let mut created_ids = Vec::new();\n        let mut skipped = 0u32;\n\n        for child in proposed {\n            // Dedup: does an existing child cover this task?\n            if existing_titles\n                .iter()\n                .any(|t| titles_match(t, &child.title.to_lowercase()))\n            {\n                tracing::debug!(\n                    parent_id = %parent.id,\n                    child_title = %child.title,\n                    \"skipping duplicate child\"\n                );\n                skipped += 1;\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            // Create child bead with parent-tracking labels for reliable dedup.\n            // Labels are stored on the bead itself and survive FrankenSQLite\n            // index corruption, unlike dependency relationships.\n            let parent_label = format!(\"parent-{}\", parent.id);\n            let labels: Vec<&str> = vec![\"mitosis-child\", \"mitosis-depth:1\", &parent_label];\n            let child_id = store\n                .create_bead(&child.title, &child.body, &labels)\n                .await\n                .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to create child bead: {}\", child.title))?;\n\n            // Link child as blocking parent.\n            store\n                .add_dependency(&child_id, &parent.id)\n                .await\n                .with_context(|| {\n                    format!(\n                        \"failed to add dependency: {} blocks {}\",\n                        child_id, parent.id\n                    )\n                })?;\n\n            tracing::info!(\n                parent_id = %parent.id,\n                child_id = %child_id,\n                child_title = %child.title,\n                \"created mitosis child\"\n            );\n\n            created_ids.push(child_id);\n        }\n\n        if created_ids.is_empty() {\n            // All proposed children already existed.\n            tracing::info!(\n                parent_id = %parent.id,\n                existing = existing.len(),\n                \"all proposed children already exist (dedup)\"\n            );\n            self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisSkipped {\n                parent_id: parent.id.clone(),\n                existing_children: existing.len() as u32,\n            })?;\n            return Ok(MitosisResult::Skipped {\n                reason: \"all children already exist\".to_string(),\n            });\n        }\n\n        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::MitosisSplit {\n            parent_id: parent.id.clone(),\n            children_created: created_ids.len() as u32,\n            children_skipped: skipped,\n            child_ids: created_ids.clone(),\n        })?;\n\n        // Record the final counts on the bead.mitosis span\n        tracing::Span::current()\n            .record(\"needle.mitosis.children_created\", created_ids.len() as u32);\n        tracing::Span::current().record(\"needle.mitosis.children_skipped\", skipped);\n\n        tracing::info!(\n            parent_id = %parent.id,\n            children_created = created_ids.len(),\n            children_skipped = skipped,\n            \"mitosis split completed\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(MitosisResult::Split {\n            children: created_ids,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Read the failure count label from a bead.\n    ///\n    /// All br calls are wrapped in timeouts to prevent indefinite hang.\n    async fn get_failure_count(&self, store: &dyn BeadStore, bead_id: &BeadId) -> Result<u32> {\n        let labels =\n            match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(30), store.labels(bead_id))\n                .await\n            {\n                Ok(Ok(l)) => l,\n                Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        bead_id = %bead_id,\n                        error = %e,\n                        \"failed to read labels for failure count\"\n                    );\n                    return Ok(0);\n                }\n                Err(_) => {\n                    tracing::warn!(\n                        bead_id = %bead_id,\n                        \"labels() timed out after 30s, assuming failure count 0\"\n                    );\n                    return Ok(0);\n                }\n            };\n\n        let count = labels\n            .iter()\n            .filter_map(|l| l.strip_prefix(\"failure-count:\"))\n            .filter_map(|n| n.parse::<u32>().ok())\n            .max()\n            .unwrap_or(0);\n\n        Ok(count)\n    }\n\n    /// Get titles of existing children for a parent bead.\n    ///\n    /// Uses label-based discovery (`parent-<parent_id>` label) instead of\n    /// reading the parent's dependency list. This is robust against\n    /// FrankenSQLite index corruption where `br dep add` creates the\n    /// dependency link and labels but the relationship doesn't appear in\n    /// `br show --json` output.\n    ///\n    /// The `list_all()` call is wrapped in a timeout to prevent indefinite\n    /// hang in HANDLING state.\n    async fn get_existing_children(\n        &self,\n        store: &dyn BeadStore,\n        parent_id: &BeadId,\n    ) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n        let parent_label = format!(\"parent-{}\", parent_id);\n        let all_beads = match tokio::time::timeout(\n            std::time::Duration::from_secs(30),\n            store.list_all(),\n        )\n        .await\n        {\n            Ok(Ok(beads)) => beads,\n            Ok(Err(e)) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    parent_id = %parent_id,\n                    error = %e,\n                    \"list_all failed during get_existing_children, assuming no children\"\n                );\n                return Ok(Vec::new());\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    parent_id = %parent_id,\n                    \"list_all timed out after 30s during get_existing_children, assuming no children\"\n                );\n                return Ok(Vec::new());\n            }\n        };\n\n        let titles: Vec<String> = all_beads\n            .iter()\n            .filter(|b| b.labels.iter().any(|l| l == &parent_label))\n            .map(|b| b.title.clone())\n            .collect();\n\n        Ok(titles)\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Parsing\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Parse the agent's mitosis analysis response from stdout.\n///\n/// Searches for a JSON object in the output. Handles markdown code fencing\n/// and surrounding text.\nfn parse_mitosis_response(stdout: &str) -> Option<MitosisResponse> {\n    // Try direct JSON parse first.\n    if let Ok(resp) = serde_json::from_str::<MitosisResponse>(stdout.trim()) {\n        return Some(resp);\n    }\n\n    // Try extracting JSON from markdown code fences.\n    let json_str = extract_json_block(stdout)?;\n    serde_json::from_str::<MitosisResponse>(json_str).ok()\n}\n\n/// Extract a JSON object from text that may contain markdown code fences.\nfn extract_json_block(text: &str) -> Option<&str> {\n    // Look for ```json ... ``` or ``` ... ``` blocks.\n    if let Some(start) = text.find(\"```json\") {\n        let content_start = start + \"```json\".len();\n        if let Some(end) = text[content_start..].find(\"```\") {\n            return Some(text[content_start..content_start + end].trim());\n        }\n    }\n\n    if let Some(start) = text.find(\"```\") {\n        let content_start = start + \"```\".len();\n        // Skip to next line if the opening ``` has text after it.\n        let content_start = text[content_start..]\n            .find('\\n')\n            .map(|n| content_start + n + 1)\n            .unwrap_or(content_start);\n        if let Some(end) = text[content_start..].find(\"```\") {\n            return Some(text[content_start..content_start + end].trim());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Try to find a bare JSON object.\n    let trimmed = text.trim();\n    if trimmed.starts_with('{') {\n        // Find matching closing brace (simple heuristic).\n        let mut depth = 0i32;\n        for (i, ch) in trimmed.char_indices() {\n            match ch {\n                '{' => depth += 1,\n                '}' => {\n                    depth -= 1;\n                    if depth == 0 {\n                        return Some(&trimmed[..=i]);\n                    }\n                }\n                _ => {}\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    None\n}\n\n/// Sanitize a path string for use as a filename component.\nfn sanitize_path_component(s: &str) -> String {\n    s.chars()\n        .map(|c| {\n            if c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '-' || c == '_' {\n                c\n            } else {\n                '_'\n            }\n        })\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// Check if two titles match (fuzzy comparison for dedup).\n///\n/// Considers titles matching if they are identical after normalization,\n/// or if one contains the other as a substring.\nfn titles_match(existing: &str, proposed: &str) -> bool {\n    if existing == proposed {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // Normalize: remove common prefixes and compare.\n    let normalize = |s: &str| -> String {\n        s.trim()\n            .trim_start_matches(|c: char| !c.is_alphabetic())\n            .to_lowercase()\n    };\n\n    let e = normalize(existing);\n    let p = normalize(proposed);\n\n    e == p || e.contains(&p) || p.contains(&e)\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::bead_store::{Filters, RepairReport};\n    use   }\n        }\n\n        fn with_labels(mut self, labels: Vec<String>) -> Self {\n            self.labels = labels;\n            self\n        }\n\n        /// Add existing child beads that will be Ok(())\n        }\n        async fn labels(&self, _id: &BeadId) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n            Ok(self.labels.clone())\n        }\n        async fn add_label(&self, _id: &BeadId, _label: &str) blocked_id.to_string()));\n            Ok(())\n        }\n        async fn remove_dependency(\n            &self,\n            _blocked_id: &BeadId,\n            _blocker_id: &BeadId,\n        ) -> Result<()> {\n            Ok(())\n        }\n        async fn doctor_repair(&self) -> Result<RepairReport> {\n            Ok(RepairReport::default())\n        }\n        async fn doctor_check(&self) -> Result<RepairReport> {\n            Ok(RepairReport::default())\n        }\n        async fn full_rebuild(&self) -> Result<()> {\n            Ok(())\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn test_bead() -> Bead {\n        Bead {\n            id: BeadId::from(\"parent-001\"),\n            title: \"Multi-task bead\".to_string(),\n            body: Some(\"Add endpoint AND write migration AND update   assert!(titles_match(\"add endpoint\", \"add endpoint for users\"));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn titles_no_match() {\n        assert!(!titles_match(\"write migration\", \"add endpoint\"));\n    }\n\n    // ── extract_json_block tests ──\n\n    #[test]\n    fn extract_from_json_fence() {\n        let text = \"blah\\n```json\\n{\\\"splittable\\\": true}\\n```\\nmore\";\n        let block = \"{\\\"splittable\\\": false}\";\n        let block = extract_json_block(text);\n        assert!(block.is_some());\n    }\n\n    // ── sanitize_path_component tests ──\n\n    #[test]\n    fn precondition tests ──\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn evaluate_skips_when_disabled() {\n        let config = MitosisConfig {\n            enabled: false,\n            first_failure_only: true,\n            force_failure_threshold: 0,\n            repeat_interval: 0,\n        };\n        let telemetry = crate::telemetry::Telemetry::new(\"test\".to_string());\n        let evaluator =    .evaluate(\n                &store,\n                &bead,\n                Path::new(\"/tmp/test\"),\n                // These won't be accessed because we skip early.\n                // Pass #[tokio::test]\n    async fn evaluate_skips_when_not_first_failure() {\n        let config = MitosisConfig {\n            enabled: true,\n            first_failure_only: true,\n            force_failure_threshold: 0,\n            repeat_interval: 0,\n        };\n        let telemetry = crate::telemetry::Telemetry::new(\"test\".to_string());\n        let evaluator = MitosisEvaluator::new(config, telemetry, PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\"));\n        let store = MockStore::new().with_labels(vec![\"failure-count:2\".to_string()]);\n        let bead = test_bead();\n\n MitosisResult::Skipped { .. }));\n    }\n\n    /// Create a bead that looks like an existing mitosis child of a parent.\n    fn existing_child(title: &str, parent_id: &str) -> Bead {\n        Bead {\n            id: BeadId::from(format!(\"existing-{}\", title.replace(' ', \"-\"))),\n            title: title.to_string(),\n            body: Some(\"Existing child\".to_string()),\n            priority: 1,\n            status: BeadStatus::Open,\n            assignee: None,\n            labels: vec![\n                \"mitosis-child\".to_string(),\n                \"mitosis-depth:1\".to_string(),\n                format!(\"parent-{}\", parent_id),\n            ],\n            workspace: PathBuf::from(\"/tmp/test\"),\n            dependencies: vec![],\n            dependents: vec![],\n            created_at: Utc::now(),\n            updated_at: Utc::now(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn create_children_with_dedup() {\n        let config = MitosisConfig {\n            enabled: true,\n            first_failure_only: true,\n            force_failure_threshold: 0,\n            repeat_interval: 0,\n        };\n        let telemetry = crate::telemetry::Telemetry::new(\"test\".to_string());\n        let evaluator = MitosisEvaluator::new(config, telemetry, PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\"));\n\n        // Parent already has a child titled \"Add endpoint\" (found via label).\n        let store = MockStore::new()\n            .with_existing_children(vec![existing_child(\"Add endpoint\", \"parent-001\")]);\n\n        let parent =
